Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The math_analysis variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 221 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 874 and intersection form of rank 3.

For the inductive step, we decompose the math_analysis structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 874.
